2. The rights of the victim and of third parties, who have not contributed to their use or production, or taken advantage of the object of which they are owners, are safeguarded.
3. The court fixes the destination of objects declared forfeited whenever the law does not do so, and may order their total or partial destruction or place them outside commerce.
4. The provisions of paragraph 1 take place even if no specific person can be punished for the act.

Article 103 Forfeiture of advantages

1. All things, rights or advantages acquired, directly or indirectly, as a consequence of the commission of a crime, are declared forfeited in favour of the State, without prejudice to the rights of the victim or of third parties in good faith.
2. If the things, rights or advantages cannot be appropriated in kind, the forfeiture is replaced by payment to the State of the respective value.

Article 104 Civil liability arising from crime

1. Compensation for losses and damages arising from a crime is mandatory and officially ascertained and awarded by the court whenever the damages have been ascertained and quantified, unless the injured party under the terms of criminal procedural law declares that he intends to bring the claim separately.
2. The conditions and calculation of compensation are governed by the rules of civil law.
3. The person responsible for compensation may effect a settlement thereof by giving notice to the court, under penalty of ineffectiveness of the act.

Article 105 Privilege of the injured party's credit

The credit arising from the injured party's right to compensation for losses and damages arising from crime benefits from preference over any other arising after the commission of the act, including costs and the amount relating to the fine.

TITLE V RIGHT TO LODGE A COMPLAINT

Article 106 Nature of crime

1. Crimes may be of a public or semi-public nature, for the purposes of exercising the right to lodge a complaint.
2. Public crimes are those whose criminal proceedings do not depend on a complaint.
3. Semi-public crimes are those whose proceedings can only be initiated after the right to lodge a complaint has been exercised.
4. The right to lodge a complaint consists in the manifestation of will by the holder thereof that he wishes criminal proceedings.

Article 107

Holder of the right to lodge a complaint

When criminal proceedings depend on a complaint, those entitled to lodge it are the holders of the right indicated in criminal procedural law.

Article 108

Time limit for exercising the right

The time limit for exercising the right to lodge a complaint is 6 months and is counted autonomously for each of the holders of the right to lodge a complaint.

Article 109

Waiver and withdrawal of complaint

The waiver, withdrawal or non-exercise of the right to lodge a complaint in relation to one of the participants in the crime benefits the others, in cases where they too cannot be prosecuted without a complaint.

TITLE VI

EXTINCTION OF CRIMINAL LIABILITY

CHAPTER I

PRESCRIPTION OF CRIMINAL PROCEEDINGS

Article 110 Limitation periods

1. Criminal proceedings are extinguished by limitation as soon as the following periods have elapsed since the commission of the crime:
a) 20 years, in the case of crimes punishable by imprisonment whose maximum limit exceeds 12 years;
b) 15 years, in the case of crimes punishable by imprisonment whose maximum limit exceeds 7 years but does not exceed 12 years;
c) 8 years, in the case of crimes punishable by imprisonment whose maximum limit exceeds 3 years but does not exceed 7 years;
d) 4 years, in all other cases.

2. Where the law provides for any crime, alternatively, imprisonment or a fine, only the former is considered for the purposes of this article.

Article 111 Calculation of the period

1. The limitation period for criminal proceedings runs from the day on which the act was completed or from the day of the last act of execution in the case of an uncompleted crime, continuing crime or habitual crime.
2. In the case of permanent crimes, the limitation period is calculated from the day on which the completion ceases.
3. In the case of complicity, the act of the principal offender is taken into account.

Article 112 Suspension of limitation

1. The limitation of criminal proceedings is suspended, in addition to cases specifically provided for by law, during the time in which:
a) The proceedings cannot legally commence or continue due to lack of legal authorisation or a judgment to be delivered by a non-criminal court, or by reason of the referral of a preliminary question to a non-criminal court;
b) The offender is serving, abroad, a sentence or custodial security measure;
c) Criminal proceedings are pending, from the moment the accused is notified of the charge.
2. The limitation period resumes from the day on which the cause of suspension ceases.
3. The cause of suspension may not, depending on the circumstances, exceed half of the period provided for in Article 110.

CHAPTER II LIMITATION OF SENTENCES AND SECURITY

MEASURES

Article 113

Limitation periods for sentences
1. Sentences are subject to limitation in the following periods:
a) 25 years if they exceed 12 years of imprisonment;
b) 20 years if they exceed 8 years but do not exceed 12 years of imprisonment;
c) 12 years if they exceed 4 years but do not exceed 8 years of imprisonment;
d) 8 years in other cases of imprisonment sentences;
e) 4 years in the case of fines.
2. The limitation period for sentences is calculated from the date on which the decision imposing it becomes final.

Article 114 Limitation of accessory sentences

The limitation of accessory sentences is subject to the regime of the principal sentence.

Article 115

Limitation periods for security measures

Security measures are subject to limitation in the following cases:
a) 15 years if custodial;
b) 5 years if non-custodial;
c) 2 years in cases of revocation of a firearms licence.

Article 116 Suspension of limitation

1. The limitation of sentences and security measures is suspended, in addition to cases specifically provided for by law, during the time in which:
a) By force of law, execution cannot begin or continue;
b) Following the escape of the convicted person and while he or she is not recaptured;
c) The convicted person is serving another sentence or custodial security measure;
d) The deferral of payment of the fine continues;

e) The convicted person is temporarily prevented from performing community service;
f) Execution is taking place.
2. The limitation period resumes from the day on which the cause of suspension ceases.
3. The provisions of paragraph 3 of Article 112 apply correspondingly.

CHAPTER III NON-LIMITATION

Article 117

Crimes of genocide, against peace and humanity and war crimes

Criminal proceedings and sentences imposed for crimes of genocide, against peace and humanity and war crimes are not subject to limitation.

CHAPTER IV

OTHER CAUSES OF EXTINCTION

Article 118 Other causes

In addition to cases specifically provided for by law, criminal liability is extinguished by the death of the agent, by amnesty and by pardon.

Article 119 Death of the agent

The death of the agent extinguishes criminal proceedings, as well as any criminal sanction that has been imposed on him or her.

Article 120 Amnesty

Amnesty extinguishes criminal proceedings and terminates the execution of the sanction not yet served in whole or in part, as well as its effects and accessory sentences to the extent possible.

Article 121 Amnesty and concurrence of crimes

Unless otherwise provided, amnesty is applicable to each of the crimes that constitute the concurrence.

Article 122 Pardon

Pardon extinguishes the sentence, in whole or in part, or replaces it with another provided for by law and more favourable to the convicted person.

BOOK II SPECIAL PART

TITLE I

CRIMES AGAINST PEACE AND HUMANITY

CHAPTER I

CRIMES OF GENOCIDE AND AGAINST HUMANITY

Article 123 Genocide

1. Whoever, with intent to destroy, in whole or in part, a national, ethnic, racial or religious group, commits:
a) Homicide or serious physical or psychological injury to members of the group;
b) Acts that by any means prevent procreation or the birth of members of the group;
c) Rape, sexual slavery, forced prostitution, forced pregnancy, forced sterilisation or any other form of sexual violence of comparable gravity;
d) Forcible separation of members of the group to another group;
e) Acts that violently prevent the group from settling or remaining in a geographical area that

is recognised as theirs by tradition or history;
f) Subjection of the group to conditions of existence or to cruel, degrading or inhuman treatment likely to bring about its destruction, in whole or in part;
g) Widespread confiscation or seizure of property belonging to members of the group;
h) Prohibition of certain commercial, industrial or professional activities to members of the group;
i) Spreading of an epidemic likely to cause death or physical injury to members of the group;
j) Prohibition, omission or prevention by any means of the provision to members of the group of humanitarian assistance adequate to combat situations of epidemic or severe food shortage;
is punished by imprisonment for 15 to 30 years.
2. Whoever publicly and directly incites the commission of genocide is punished by imprisonment for 5 to 15 years.

Article 124

Crimes against humanity

Whoever, in the context of a widespread or systematic attack against any civilian population, commits acts resulting in:
a)	Homicide or serious physical or psychological harm;
b)	Extermination, understood as the subjection of all or part of the population to adverse living conditions, such as deprivation of access to food or medicine, capable of causing the death of one or more persons;
c)	Enslavement;
d)	Deportation or forcible transfer of a population, understood as the unlawful displacement of one or more persons to another State or location through their expulsion or other coercive act;

e)	Imprisonment or any other severe form of deprivation of physical liberty of a person, in violation of the norms or principles of international law;
f)	Torture, understood as the act consisting of inflicting severe pain or suffering, physical or psychological, on a person deprived of liberty or under the control of the agent;
g)	Rape, sexual slavery, forced prostitution, forced pregnancy, forced sterilisation or any other form of sexual violence of comparable gravity;
h)	Persecution, understood as the deprivation of the enjoyment of fundamental rights, in violation of international law, of a group or collectivity that can be identified on political, racial, national, ethnic, cultural, religious, gender grounds or on the basis of other grounds universally recognised as unacceptable under international law;
i)	Enforced disappearance of persons, understood as the detention, imprisonment or abduction promoted by a State or political organisation, or with its authorisation, support or acquiescence, followed by a refusal to acknowledge such state of deprivation of liberty or to provide any information on the situation or location of those persons, with the purpose of denying them the protection of the law for a prolonged period of time;
j)	Apartheid, understood as any inhumane act committed in the context of an institutionalised regime of systematic oppression and domination by one racial group over another or others, with the intention of maintaining that regime;
k)	Other inhumane acts of a similar character intentionally causing great suffering, serious injury or affecting mental or physical health;
is punished with imprisonment from 15 to 30 years.

CHAPTER II

WAR CRIMES

Article 125

War crimes against persons

1.	Whoever, in the context of an international armed conflict or non-international armed conflict, against a person protected by international humanitarian law, commits:
a)	Homicide;
b)	Torture or cruel, degrading or inhumane treatment including biological experiments;
c)	Serious physical harm or acts causing great suffering;
d)	Taking of hostages;
e)	Compelling to serve in enemy armed forces or proceeding to recruit or enlist children under 18 years of age in armed forces, military or paramilitary forces of a State, or in armed groups distinct from the armed forces, military or paramilitary forces of a State, or their use to participate in hostilities;
f)	Severe, prolonged and unjustified restrictions on the liberty of persons;
g)	Deportation or transfer, or unlawful deprivation of liberty;
h)	Unjustified removal or destruction of property of great value;
i)	Conviction and execution of sentence, without prior fair and impartial trial;
j)	Acts that outrage the dignity of the human person, in particular by means of humiliating and degrading treatment;
k)	Homicide or injuries inflicted on a combatant who has laid down arms or who, having no means to defend himself, has unconditionally surrendered or has in any way been placed out of combat;
l)	The acts described in paragraph g) of the preceding article;

m)	Subjecting persons who are under the control of a belligerent party to physical mutilations or to any type of medical or scientific experiments that are not motivated by medical, dental or hospital treatment, nor carried out in the interest of those persons, and which cause death or seriously endanger their health;
is punished with imprisonment from 12 to 25 years.
2.	The penalty is aggravated by one fifth in its limits when the acts referred to in the preceding paragraph are committed against members of a humanitarian institution.
3.	Whoever, in the context of an international armed conflict:
a)	Transfers, directly or indirectly, as an occupying power, part of its own civilian population into the occupied territory or transfers all or part of the population of the occupied territory, within or outside that territory;
b)	Compels a prisoner of war or other protected person to serve in the armed forces of an enemy power;
c)	After the cessation of hostilities, delays, without justified reason, the repatriation of prisoners of war;
is punished with imprisonment from 15 to 30 years.

Article 126

War crimes by use of prohibited methods of warfare

Whoever, in the context of an international armed conflict or non-international armed conflict:
a)	Attacks the civilian population in general or civilians not taking direct part in hostilities;
b)	Attacks civilian objects, that is, objects that are not military objectives;
c)	Attacks,	by	any	means,	population centres, dwellings or buildings that are not

defended and that are not military objectives;
d)	Launches an indiscriminate attack affecting the civilian population or civilian objects, knowing that such attack causes loss of human life, injury to civilian persons or damage to civilian objects, which are excessive;
e)	Takes advantage of the presence of civilians or other protected persons to prevent certain points, areas or military forces from being the target of military operations;
f)	Deliberately causes starvation of the civilian population as a method of warfare, depriving it of goods indispensable to its survival;
g)	Declares or threatens, in the capacity of an officer, that no quarter will be given;
h)	Kills or injures enemy combatants treacherously;
i)	Launches an attack, being able to know that it causes extensive, lasting and severe damage to the environment which is clearly excessive in relation to the concrete and direct overall military advantage anticipated;
j)	Commits perfidy, understood as the act of killing, injuring or capturing, which appeals, with intent to deceive, to the good faith of an adversary to make him believe that he has the right to receive, or the obligation to ensure the protection provided by the rules of international humanitarian law;
is punished with imprisonment from 15 to 30 years.

Artigo 127º

War crimes by use of prohibited means of warfare

1.	Whoever, in the context of an international armed conflict or a non-international armed conflict, employs weapons, projectiles, materials and methods of combat which, by their very nature, cause superfluous injury or unnecessary suffering or which have indiscriminate effects,

in violation of international law applicable to armed conflicts, is punished with imprisonment from 12 to 25 years.
2.	The preceding paragraph covers, in particular, the use of:
a)	Poison or poisoned weapons;
b)	Asphyxiating, toxic or similar gases or any analogous liquid, material or device;
c)	Bullets which expand or flatten easily in the human body, such as bullets with a hard casing which does not entirely cover the core or has incisions;
d)	Anti-personnel mines;
e)	Chemical weapons;
f)	Weapons the primary effect of which is to injure by fragments not detectable by X-rays in the human body;
g)	Incendiary weapons;
h)	Laser weapons causing blindness.
3.	The weapons, instruments and products referred to in the preceding paragraph are those considered as such by international law.

Artigo 128º

War crimes against property protected by distinctive insignia or emblems

Whoever, in the context of an international armed conflict or a non-international armed conflict, attacks:
a)	Personnel, installations, material, units or vehicles involved in a peacekeeping or humanitarian assistance mission in accordance with the Charter of the United Nations, provided that they are entitled to the protection given to civilians or civilian objects under international humanitarian law;
b)	Buildings, installations, material, units or vehicles, duly marked with the distinctive emblems of the Geneva Conventions

or	personnel	entitled	to	use	the	same emblems.
is punished with imprisonment from 10 to 20 years.

Artigo 129º

War crimes against property

Whoever, in the context of an international armed conflict or in the context of a non-international armed conflict:
a)	Steals, destroys or damages property on a large scale or of great value, without military necessity or in an unlawful and arbitrary manner;
b)	Attacks, destroys or damages buildings dedicated to religious worship, education, the arts, sciences or charitable purposes, cultural or historical monuments, archaeological sites, hospitals and places where the sick and wounded are gathered, provided that they are not military objectives;
c)	Pillages a town or locality, even when taken by assault;
is punished with imprisonment from 5 to 15 years.

Artigo 130º

War crimes against other rights

Whoever, in the context of an international armed conflict or in the context of a non-international armed conflict, declares abolished, suspended or inadmissible in a court of law, any rights and proceedings of nationals of the adverse party is punished with imprisonment from 5 to 15 years.

CAPÍTULO III AGAINST PEACE AND FREEDOM

Artigo 131º Terrorist organisations

1.	A terrorist group, organisation or association is considered to be a grouping of two or more persons who, in order to achieve political, ideological, philosophical or religious purposes, act in concert, aiming to harm national integrity or independence, prevent, alter or subvert the functioning of national or international institutions, intimidate or coerce public authority, international organisations or certain persons, groups of persons or the general population, to perform an act, to refrain from performing it or to tolerate that it be performed, by committing serious crimes:
a)	Against the life, physical integrity or freedom of persons;
b)	Against the security of transport and communications, including telegraphic, telephonic, radio or television;
c)	Of intentional production of common danger, through fire, explosion, release of radioactive substances or toxic or asphyxiating gases, flood or avalanche, collapse of construction, contamination of food and water intended for human consumption or spread of disease, plague, harmful plant or animal;
d)	Involving the use of nuclear energy, firearms, explosive substances or devices, incendiary means of any nature, parcels or letter bombs.
e)	Acts that destroy or render inoperative or divert from their normal purposes, permanently or temporarily, in whole or in part, means or routes of communication, public service facilities or international facilities or facilities intended for the supply and satisfaction of the vital needs of the population.
f)	Research and development of biological or chemical weapons
2.	Whoever promotes or founds a terrorist group, organisation or association, joins, participates in or supports them, is punished with imprisonment from 12 to 25 years.

3.	Whoever leads or directs a terrorist group, organisation or association is punished with imprisonment from 15 to 30 years.
4.	When a terrorist group, organisation or association, or the persons referred to in paragraphs 2 or 3, possess any of the means indicated in subparagraph d) of paragraph 1, the penalty is increased by one third in its minimum and maximum limits.
5.	Preparatory acts for the establishment of a terrorist group, organisation or association are punished with the penalty reduced by half in its minimum and maximum limits.
6.	The penalties referred to may be extraordinarily mitigated or punishment may not take place if the offender voluntarily abandons his activity, prevents or seriously endeavours to prevent the danger caused by it or the continuation of terrorist groups, organisations or associations, or communicates their existence to the authority so that it can prevent the commission of crimes.

Artigo 132º Terrorism

1.	Whoever commits any of the crimes provided for in subparagraphs a) to c) and e) of paragraph 1 of the preceding article, or any crime with the use of the means referred to in subparagraphs d) or f) of the same provision, with the intention referred to therein, is punished with imprisonment from 12 to 25 years, or with the penalty corresponding to the crime committed, increased by one third in its minimum and maximum limits, if the penalty is equal or higher.
2.	The penalty may be extraordinarily mitigated or punishment may not take place if the offender voluntarily abandons his activity, removes or considerably reduces the danger caused by it, prevents the result that the law seeks to avoid from occurring, or concretely assists in the collection of decisive evidence for the identification or capture of other responsible persons.

Artigo 133º Financing of terrorism

Whoever by any means, directly or indirectly and intentionally, provides, collects or holds funds or property of any kind, as well as products or rights capable of being converted into funds, attempts to do so, with the intention that they be used or knowing that they may be used, in whole or in part, in the planning, preparation or commission of the acts provided for in paragraph 1 of article 131º, or commits such acts with the intention referred to in paragraph 1 of article 132º, is punished with imprisonment from 12 to 25 years.

Artigo 134º Incitement to war

1.	Whoever, by any means, publicly and repeatedly, incites hatred against a race, a people or a nation, with the intention of provoking a war or preventing peaceful coexistence between different races, peoples or nations, is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years.
2.	Whoever entices or recruits Timorese or foreign citizens to, in the service of a foreign group or power, wage war against a State or to overthrow the legitimate Government of another State by violent means, is punished with imprisonment from 5 to 15 years.

Article 135 Racial or religious discrimination

1. Whoever founds, establishes an organisation or develops organised propaganda activities that incite or encourage racial or religious discrimination, hatred or violence, as well as whoever participates in or provides assistance, including financing, to the organisation or the activities referred to, is punished with a prison sentence of 4 to 12 years.
2. Whoever, at a public meeting, through writing intended for dissemination or through any means of social communication, disseminates ideas with the intention of inciting racial or religious discrimination or encouraging it, or provoking acts of violence against a person or group of

persons because of their race, colour, ethnic origin or religion, is punished with a prison sentence of 2 to 8 years.

CHAPTER IV COMMON PROVISIONS

Article 136

Responsibility of military commanders and other superiors

1. A military commander or person acting as such who, having knowledge that the forces under his effective command and control or under his effective responsibility and control are committing any of the crimes provided for in this title, does not adopt all necessary and adequate measures to prevent or suppress their commission or to bring it to the immediate attention of the competent authorities, is punished with the penalty corresponding to the crime or crimes that are actually committed.
2. The provisions of the preceding paragraph apply, with the necessary adaptations, to a superior with respect to the control of subordinates under his effective authority and control.

Article 137 Definitions

For the purposes of this Title, the following are considered:
a) "Armed conflict of an international character", one that:
i) Occurs between States, even without a formal declaration of war, even if the state of war is not recognised by one of them;
ii) Corresponds to a situation of total or partial occupation of the territory of a State, even if that occupation does not encounter any military resistance;
iii) Is subsumed in a situation in which peoples fight against colonial domination, foreign occupation and against segregation regimes, in the exercise of the right of peoples to self-determination,

enshrined in the Charter of the United Nations and in the declaration concerning the principles of international law with respect to friendly relations and cooperation among States;
b) "Armed conflict of a non-international character", one that takes place in the territory of a State, is of a protracted nature and opposes governmental authorities and organised armed groups or these among themselves, with the exception of situations of internal disturbances and tensions, such as sporadic or isolated acts of violence or others of a similar nature;
c) "Protected persons":
i) In international armed conflicts, persons protected for the purposes of the Geneva Conventions of 1949 and Additional Protocol I, namely the wounded, sick, shipwrecked, prisoners of war, medical or religious personnel and civilian population;
ii) In armed conflict of a non-international character, the wounded, the sick, the shipwrecked, as well as persons not taking active part in hostilities in the power of the enemy;
iii) In armed conflict of an international character and in armed conflict of a non-international character, members of the armed forces and combatants of the enemy party who have laid down their arms or have no other means of defence.

TITLE II

CRIMES AGAINST PERSONS

CHAPTER I AGAINST LIFE

Article 138 Simple homicide

Whoever kills another person is punished with a prison sentence of 8 to 20 years.

Article 139 Aggravated homicide

If death is caused in circumstances that reveal special culpability or perversity, namely:
a) With the use of poison, torture, asphyxiation, fire, explosive or other insidious means or that results in the commission of a crime of common danger, or, further, with another act of cruelty to increase the suffering of the victim;

b) Through dissimulation or other means or recourse that makes defence by the victim difficult or impossible;
c) Out of greed, for the pleasure of killing, for excitement or to satisfy sexual instinct, by payment or reward or its promise, or for any other futile or base motive;
d) With the purpose of preparing, executing or concealing another crime, facilitating the escape or ensuring the impunity of the perpetrator of a crime;
e) Out of racial, religious or political hatred;
f) With premeditation, this being understood as coldness of mind, reflection on the means employed or postponement of the intention to kill for more than 24 hours;
g) If the victim is a spouse, descendant, ascendant, collateral, related family member up to the second degree, adoptee of the perpetrator or person who lives with him in analogous conditions where there is a hierarchical, economic or employment dependence;
h) If the victim is a particularly vulnerable person by reason of age, illness or physical or mental disability;
i) If the victim is a member of a sovereign body and constitutional political bodies, member of a body of local administration, magistrate,

defender, lawyer, court officer, civil servant or any other person entrusted with a public service, provided it is in the exercise of or because of the exercise of their functions;
j) If the victim is a witness, declarant, expert, injured party or offended party and the crime is committed with the purpose of preventing testimony, the denunciation of facts or the filing of a complaint or because of their intervention in the proceedings.
the perpetrator is punished with a prison sentence of 12 to 25 years.

Article 140 Negligent homicide

1. Whoever, through negligence, kills another person, is punished with a prison sentence of up to 4 years or a fine.
2. In cases where the perpetrator has acted with gross negligence, he is punished with a prison sentence of up to 5 years.

Artigo 141º* Interrupção da gravidez

1.	Whoever, by any means and without the consent of the pregnant woman, causes her to abort is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years.
2.	Whoever, by any means and with the consent of the pregnant woman, causes her to abort is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years.
3.	The pregnant woman who gives consent to the abortion performed by a third party, or who, by her own act or that of another, causes herself to abort, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years.
4.	The provisions of the preceding paragraphs do not apply when the termination of pregnancy constitutes, in accordance with the state of medical knowledge and experience, and after all possible actions have been carried out to save the life of the pregnant woman and that of the fetus or embryo, the only means to remove danger of death to the pregnant woman, provided it is performed, upon medical certification, by a doctor or other health professional under his direction in a public health establishment or

officially recognized, and with the consent of the pregnant woman.
5.	The verification of the circumstances referred to in the preceding paragraph is certified in a medical certificate written and signed by a panel of three doctors, on a date prior to that of the termination, of which the doctor who performs or supervises the performance of the termination of pregnancy is not a part.
6.	Consent is given in writing in a document signed by the pregnant woman – after hearing, if possible, the spouse or the person who lives with her in conditions analogous to those of spouses – or by another person on her behalf, whenever possible with two days' notice in relation to the date of termination.
7.	In the case where the pregnant woman is a minor, consent is given by the legal representative.
8.	In the case where the pregnant woman who is of age or emancipated is or becomes psychically incapable, consent is given, respectively and successively, by the spouse or the person who lives with her in conditions analogous to those of spouses, by the legal representative, by an ascendant or descendant, or, in their absence, by any relatives of the collateral line.
9.	When it is not possible to obtain the medical certificate referred to in paragraph 5, and/or it is not possible to obtain consent under the terms of paragraphs 6 to 8, and the performance of the termination is urgent and cannot be postponed, the doctor decides in conscience in view of the situation, resorting, whenever possible, to the opinion of other doctors.
10.	The panel referred to in paragraph 5 is constituted by doctors possessing adequate knowledge for the assessment of the circumstances that justify the termination of pregnancy, including, whenever possible, a gynecologist/obstetrician.
11.	The doctors who issue the medical certificate and the doctors who perform or supervise the performance of the termination of pregnancy must provide all necessary clarifications to the pregnant woman and, if applicable, to the persons referred to in paragraphs 7 and 8, namely the method of termination to be used, the effects of the termination, the possible consequences for the physical and psychological health of the pregnant woman.

12.	Doctors and other health professionals are recognized the right to conscientious objection with respect to any acts relating to the termination of pregnancy.
13.	Doctors and other health professionals who invoke conscientious objection must guarantee immediate intervention in the necessary acts, as well as the immediate accompaniment of the pregnant woman, by another health professional.
14.	Conscientious objection is expressed and communicated, through a written and signed document, to the clinical head of the health establishment where the objector provides service.

Redacção introduzida pela lei 6/2009, de 15 de Julho A anterior redacção do artigo 141º era a seguinte:

Artigo 141º Interrupção da gravidez

1.	Whoever, by any means and without the consent of the pregnant woman, causes her to abort is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years.
2.	Whoever, by any means and with the consent of the pregnant woman, causes her to abort is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years.
3.	The pregnant woman who gives consent to the abortion performed by a third party, or who, by her own act or that of another, causes herself to abort, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years.
4.	The provisions of the preceding paragraphs do not apply to cases in which the termination of pregnancy constitutes the only means to remove danger of death or of serious and irreversible injury to the body or to the physical or psychological health of the pregnant woman or of the fetus, provided it is performed, upon authorization and supervision of a medical board, by a doctor or health professional in a public health establishment and with the consent of the pregnant woman and or of the spouse.
5.	The provisions of paragraph 4 of this article are the subject of autonomous legislation.

Artigo 142º Infanticídio

The mother who kills her child during childbirth or shortly thereafter and still under its disturbing influence is punished with imprisonment from 3 to 10 years.

Artigo 143º Exposição ou abandono

1.	Whoever, intentionally, endangers the life of another person:
a)	By exposing them in a place that subjects them to a situation from which they alone cannot defend themselves; or
b)	By abandoning them without defense, by reason of age, physical disability or illness, whenever the agent has the duty to guard, watch over or assist them;
is punished with imprisonment from 1 to 6 years
2.	If from the act results:
a)	A serious offense to physical integrity, the agent is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years;
b)	Death, the agent is punished with imprisonment from 5 to 15 years.
3.	If the victim is the spouse, descendant, ascendant, collateral, affine up to the second degree, adopter or adoptee of the agent or person who lives with him in conditions analogous to those of spouses, the penalties referred to in the preceding paragraphs are increased by one third in their limits.

Artigo 144º Incitamento ou auxílio ao suicídio

1.	Whoever incites another person to commit suicide, or provides them with assistance for that purpose, if the suicide actually comes to be attempted or consummated, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.
2.	If the acts described in the preceding paragraph have as addressees any of the persons referred to in paragraph 3 of the preceding article or a minor under 17 years of age or a person whose capacity for valuation or determination is significantly diminished, the penalty is imprisonment up to 5 years.

3.	Whoever, in any form and repeatedly makes public propaganda of suicide, is punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.

CAPÍTULO II

CONTRA A INTEGRIDADE FÍSICA

Artigo 145º

Ofensas à integridade física simples

1.	Whoever offends the body or health of another person is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.
2.	Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

Artigo 146º

Ofensas à integridade física graves

Whoever offends the body or health of another person with the purpose of:
a)	Depriving of an important organ or limb;
b)	Disfiguring seriously and permanently;
c)	Affecting the capacity to work, the intellectual capacities, or of procreation in a serious and lasting manner or permanently;
d)	Provoking permanent illness or incurable psychological anomaly; or
e)	Creating danger to life;
is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years.

Artigo 147º Agravação

1.	Whoever, intending only to offend the body or health of another person:
a)	Causes the offenses provided for in artigo 146º is punished with imprisonment up to 5 years;
b)	Causes death by negligence, is punished with imprisonment from 1 to 6 years.
2.	Whoever, intending to cause any of the offenses provided for in artigo 146º, by negligence comes to cause death is punished with imprisonment from 4 to 12 years.

3.	If the crimes referred to in the two preceding articles have as victims any of the persons mentioned in paragraph i) of artigo 139º, because of or in the exercise of the functions enumerated, the penalty shall be increased by one third in its maximum and minimum limits, if more serious punishment is not due by virtue of another legal provision.

Artigo 148º

Ofensas à integridade física negligentes

1.	Whoever, through negligence, injures the body or health of another person, is punished with imprisonment up to 1 year or a fine.
2.	If the negligence is gross, the offender is punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or with a fine.
3.	If the act results in serious bodily injuries, the offender is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or with a fine.
4.	Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

Artigo 149º

Intervenções e tratamentos médico-cirúrgicos

1.	Interventions and other treatments which, according to the state of medical knowledge and experience, are indicated and are carried out, in accordance with the leges artis, by a doctor or other person legally authorized to undertake them with the intention of preventing, diagnosing, combating or alleviating a disease, suffering, injury or bodily fatigue or a mental disturbance are not considered bodily injuries.
2.	If the violation of the leges artis results in danger to the body, health or life of the patient, the offender shall be punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.
3.	Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

Artigo 150º

Ofensas por meio de substâncias venenosas
1.	Whoever injures the body or health of another by administering poisonous substances or substances harmful to

physical or mental health is punished with imprisonment up to 5 years.
2.	If the act results in any of the consequences provided for in Article 146º or the death of the victim, the offender is punished, respectively, with imprisonment from 2 to 6 years and from 4 to 12 years.

Artigo 151º

Ofensas corporais recíprocas

1.	When two persons mutually injure each other in body or health, neither acting in self-defense and none of the effects provided for in Article 146º occurring nor the death of any of the participants, they are punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.
2.	Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

Artigo 152º Participação em rixa

1.	Whoever intervenes or takes part in a brawl with two or more persons, from which death or serious bodily injury results, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine if those effects cannot be intentionally imputed to him.
2.	Participation in a brawl is not punishable when it is determined by a non-censurable motive, namely if it aims to react against an attack, defend another person or separate the combatants.

Artigo 153º Maus-tratos a incapaz

1.	Whoever has under his guard or care, or under the responsibility of his education, or, further, as a subordinate in work activity, an incapable person, particularly vulnerable by reason of illness, advanced age, pregnancy, physical or mental disability, and causes injuries to the body or health, or inflicts physical or psychological ill-treatment, or cruel treatment, is punished

with imprisonment from 2 to 6 years, if a more severe penalty is not applicable by virtue of another legal provision.
2.	If the victim is a descendant, collateral, relative or affine up to the second degree, adoptee of the offender or person who cohabits with him in analogous conditions, the penalty referred to in the preceding number is increased by one third in its limits.

Artigo 154º Maus-tratos a cônjuge

Whoever inflicts on his spouse or on a person with whom he cohabits in a situation analogous to that of spouses physical or psychological ill-treatment or cruel treatment is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 6 years, if a more severe penalty is not applicable under another legal provision.

Artigo 155º Maus-tratos a menor

1.	Whoever, having under his guard or care a minor under 17 years of age, is responsible for his education or has him as a subordinate in work activity, and:
a)	Causes injuries to the body or health, inflicts physical or psychological ill-treatment, or cruel treatment
b)	Subjects him to economic exploitation, to dangerous work or work capable of compromising his education or his physical, mental, spiritual, moral or social development;
c)	Subjects him to any form of slavery or analogous practice;
d)	Uses, recruits or offers him for purposes of prostitution, production of pornographic material or pornographic shows; or
e)	Uses, recruits or offers him for the practice of illicit acts or activities, namely for the production and trafficking of narcotics as defined by international conventions;
is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 6 years, if a more severe penalty is not applicable by virtue of another legal provision.

2.	Whoever, in the same situation, uses the minor for begging, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years, if a more severe penalty is not applicable by virtue of another legal provision.
3.	If the victim is a descendant, collateral, relative or affine up to the second degree, adoptee of the offender or person who cohabits with him in analogous conditions, the penalties referred to in the preceding numbers are increased by one third in their limits.

Artigo 156º Agravação pelo resultado

If, as a consequence of the ill-treatment described in Articles 153º to 155º, the effects referred to in Article 146º occur, the offender is punished with imprisonment from 3 to 10 years and if death is caused the penalty is from 5 to 15 years of imprisonment.

CAPÍTULO III

CONTRA A LIBERDADE PESSOAL

SECÇÃO I AGRESSÕES PESSOAIS

Artigo 157º Ameaças

1.	Whoever, by any means, threatens another person with the commission of a crime in such a way as to cause him fear or unease or to prejudice his freedom of determination, is punished with imprisonment up to 1 year or a fine.
2.	Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

Artigo 158º Coacção

1.	Whoever, by means of violence or threat of significant harm, compels another person to an action or omission, or to endure an activity, is punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.

2.	Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

Artigo 159º Coacção Grave

If the coercion is carried out:
a)	By means of the threat of a crime punishable with imprisonment exceeding 3 years,
b)	By an official seriously abusing his functions,
c)	Against a particularly defenseless person, by reason of age, disability, illness or pregnancy
d)	Against any of the persons referred to in paragraph i) of Article 139º
the offender is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.

Artigo 160º Sequestro

1.	Whoever, outside the cases provided for in criminal procedural law, detains, imprisons, keeps imprisoned or detained another person, or in any other way deprives him of liberty, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or with a fine.
2.	The penalty of imprisonment is from 2 to 8 years if the deprivation of liberty:
a)	Lasts more than seventy-two hours;
b)	Is effected by means of injury to physical integrity, torture or any other cruel, degrading or inhuman treatment;
c)	Causes, through the negligence of the offender, the death of the victim, or has as a result the suicide of the latter;
d)	Has as victim one who exercises public, religious or political authority.
e)	Is promoted, authorized or supported by an agent of public authority or member of a political organization.

Artigo 161º Rapto

1.	Whoever, by means of violence, threat or cunning, transfers from one place to another, another person with the intention of:
a)	Subjecting the victim to extortion;
b)	Committing a crime of aggression, exploitation or sexual abuse;
c)	Obtaining ransom or reward; or
d)	Compelling the public authority or a third party to perform an action or omission, or to endure an activity;
is punished with imprisonment from 4 to 12 years.
2.	The applicable penalty is from 5 to 15 years of imprisonment if any of the circumstances provided for in number 2 of Article 160º is verified.

Article 162 Slavery

1.	Whoever, by any means, places another human being in a situation of slavery and makes use of them in that condition, is punished with a prison sentence of 8 to 20 years.
2.	The consent of the victim is irrelevant, if any of the means referred to in the following article have been used.
3.	For the purposes of applying the provisions of this article, a person is considered to be in a situation of slavery when in a state of submission, even if de facto, to powers corresponding to those of a right of ownership, or of any real right, or bound to the destination of a thing.

Article 163 Trafficking in persons

1.	Whoever recruits, alienates, transfers, acquires, transports, conveys, lodges or harbours persons, by resorting to threat, use of force or other forms of coercion, abduction, fraud, deception, abuse of authority or of a situation of vulnerability, or by means of the giving or acceptance of payments or benefits, to obtain the consent of a person having authority over another, in order to

achieve the purposes of exploitation, is punished with a prison sentence of 8 to 20 years.
2.	The penalty provided for in the preceding paragraph is incurred by whoever recruits, transports, conveys, lodges or harbours a minor under 17 years of age for purposes of exploitation even if it does not involve any of the means referred to in the preceding paragraph.
3.	For the purposes of applying the provisions of this article, exploitation must include, at a minimum, the exploitation of the prostitution of others or other forms of sexual exploitation, forced labour or services, slavery or practices similar to slavery, servitude or the extraction of organs.
4.	The consent of the victim is irrelevant, if any of the means referred to in paragraph 1 have been used.

Article 164 Aggravation

If the acts described in articles 162 and 163 are committed:
a)	As a way of facilitating the exploitation or sexual use of the victim, by the agent or by a third party;
b)	The victim being a minor under 17 years of age;
c)	The victim being in a foreign country or being moved there;
d)	Using the victim, against their will, in the commission of crimes; or
e)	The agent performing an activity that confers upon them public or religious authority before a group, region or the entirety of the country;
the agent is punished with imprisonment of 12 to 25 years.

Article 165 Trafficking in human organs

1.	Whoever obtains, alienates, transfers, acquires, transports or conveys tissues, organs, substances or parts of the human body of a third party, without consent or by resorting to threat, use of force or other forms of coercion, abduction, fraud, deception, abuse of authority or of a situation of vulnerability, or by means of the giving or

acceptance of payments or benefits, or assists in their obtaining, transaction, transport or storage, is punished with a prison sentence of 3 to 10 years.
2.	If from the commission of the acts referred to in the preceding paragraph any of the consequences provided for in article 146 or the death of the victim results, the agent is punished with a prison sentence of 4 to 12 years and 5 to 20 years, respectively.
3.	The consent of the victim is criminally irrelevant, if any of the means referred to in paragraph 1 has been used.

Article 166 Sale of persons

1.	Whoever, outside the situations provided for in article 163, by any act or other form of transaction, transfers a person or group of persons to another person or group of persons by means of payment of any amount or other consideration, reward or advantage, is punished with a prison sentence of 2 to 8 years;
2.	If the acts referred to in the preceding paragraph are committed:
a)	In relation to a minor under 17 years of age
b)	Abusing authority resulting from a family relationship, guardianship or curatorship, hierarchical, economic or work dependence;
c)	Taking advantage of the functions or position that, in any capacity, they exercise in a prison establishment, educational or correctional establishment, hospital, hospice, asylum, clinic or other health establishment or other establishment intended for assistance or treatment; or
d)	Upon an unconscious or incapable person, particularly vulnerable by reason of illness, physical or mental disability
the agent is punished with a prison sentence of 4 to 12 years.
3.	The consent of the person themselves or of a third party exercising any form of authority over the victim is criminally irrelevant.

Article 167

Torture or other cruel, degrading or inhuman treatment

1.	Whoever, having the function of prevention, investigation, decision, in relation to any type of offence, the execution of the respective sanctions or the protection, custody, surveillance or supervision of a detained or imprisoned person and tortures or treats in a cruel, degrading or inhuman manner, in order to:
a)	Obtain from them or from another person a confession, testimony, statement or information
b)	Punish them for an act committed or allegedly committed by them or by another,
c)	Intimidate them or to intimidate another person, is punished with a prison sentence of 2 to 8 years.
2.	The penalty provided for in the preceding paragraph is also incurred by whoever, on their own initiative, by order of a superior or in agreement with the competent authority to exercise the functions referred to in the preceding paragraph, assumes de facto the performance of those functions by committing any of the acts described therein.
3.	Torture, cruel, degrading or inhuman treatment is considered to be the act consisting of inflicting acute physical or psychological suffering, severe physical or psychological fatigue or the use of chemicals, drugs or other means, natural or artificial, with the intention of disturbing the capacity for decision or free expression of will of the victim.

Article 168 Aggravation

1.	Whoever, under the terms and conditions described in the preceding article:
a)	Produces serious bodily injury, under the terms provided for in article 146;
b)	Employs particularly serious means or methods of torture, namely beating, electroshock, mock

execution, hallucinogenic substances, sexual abuse or serious threat against family members;
c)	Commits such acts as a way of preventing or hindering the free exercise of political or associative rights constitutionally enshrined;
d)	Habitually commits the acts referred to therein; is punished with a prison sentence of 5 to 15 years.
2.	If from the acts referred to in this article or in the preceding article the suicide or death of the victim results, the agent is punished with a prison sentence of 5 to 20 years.

Article 169 Failure to report

1.	The hierarchical superior who, having knowledge of the commission, by a subordinate, of any of the acts described in articles 167 and 168, does not make a report within three days immediately following knowledge thereof, is punished with a prison sentence of 1 to 6 years.
2.	Anyone who, for professional reasons and officially is given knowledge of the commission of acts described in articles 167 and 168 and does not immediately communicate to the hierarchical superior or make the respective report, is punished with the penalty set out in the preceding paragraph extraordinarily mitigated.

Article 170

Freedom of assembly or demonstration

1.	Whoever interferes with an assembly or demonstration in a public place or open to the public, legally authorised, preventing or attempting to prevent its realisation is punished with a prison sentence of up to 2 years or a fine.
2.	The police authority agent who prevents or attempts to prevent, outside the legal conditions, the exercise of the right of assembly or demonstration described in the preceding paragraph, is punished with a prison sentence of up to 3 years.

SECTION II SEXUAL ASSAULTS

Article 171 Sexual coercion

Whoever, by means of violence, serious threat, or after, for that purpose, having rendered them unconscious or placed them in the impossibility of resisting, compels another person to suffer or to commit, with them or with another, a sexual act of relevance is punished with a prison sentence of 2 to 8 years.

Article 172 Rape

Any person who, by the means referred to in the previous article, engages in vaginal intercourse, anal intercourse or oral intercourse with another person or compels them to endure the introduction of objects into the anus or vagina is punished with imprisonment from 5 to 15 years.

Article 173 Aggravation

If the sexual assaults referred to in articles 171 and 172 are committed:
a) Abusing authority resulting from a family relationship, guardianship or curatorship, hierarchical, economic or employment dependence;
b) Taking advantage of the functions or position that, in any capacity, is exercised in a prison establishment, educational or correctional establishment, hospital, hospice, asylum, clinic or other health establishment or other establishment intended for assistance or treatment;
c) Against an unconscious or incapable person, particularly vulnerable by reason of illness, physical or mental disability;
d) Having as victims minors under 17 years of age;
the offender is punished with imprisonment from 4 to 12 years, in the case of article 171, and with imprisonment from 5 to 20 years, in the case of article 172.

SECTION III

SEXUAL EXPLOITATION

Article 174 Sexual exploitation of third parties

1. Whoever, with intent to profit or making it a way of life, promotes, facilitates or in any way contributes to another person engaging in prostitution or committing other sexual acts is punished with imprisonment from 3 to 10 years.
2. The offender is punished with imprisonment from 4 to 12 years, if any of the following circumstances occur:
a) Exploitation of a situation of abandonment or economic need of the victim;
b) Exercise of violence, serious threat or coercion against the victim;
c) Movement of the victim to a country different from that in which they were born or in which they resided;
d) Retention of any identification document of the victim.

Article 175 Child prostitution

1. Whoever, even with the consent of the victim, commits acts of sexual exploitation referred to in the previous article in relation to a minor under 17 years of age, is punished with imprisonment from 4 to 12 years in the case of number 1 and with imprisonment from 5 to 15 years in cases where any of the circumstances set out in number 2 of the previous article occur.
2. Whoever offers, obtains, seeks or delivers a minor under 17 years of age for purposes of child prostitution is punished with imprisonment from 4 to 12 years, if a more severe penalty is not applicable by virtue of another legal provision.

Article 176 Child pornography

1. Whoever uses, for predominantly sexual purposes, exposes or represents a minor under 17 years of age in the performance of any sexual activity, real or

simulated, or by any other means exhibits that sexual activity or the sexual organs of the minor, is punished with imprisonment from 3 to 10 years.
2. The same penalty applies to whoever produces, distributes, disseminates, imports, exports, offers, sells or possesses any means of communication, instrument, document or record for the purposes referred to in the previous number or with the purpose of divulging such acts.

SECTION IV SEXUAL ABUSE

Article 177 Sexual abuse of a minor

1. Whoever commits vaginal intercourse, anal intercourse or oral intercourse with a minor under 14 years of age is punished with imprisonment from 5 to 20 years.
2. Whoever commits any relevant sexual act with a minor under 14 years of age is punished with imprisonment from 5 to 15 years.

Article 178

Sexual acts with adolescents

Whoever, being an adult and outside the situations provided for in this section, commits any relevant sexual act with a minor between 14 and 16 years of age, abusing their inexperience, is punished with imprisonment up to 5 years.

Article 179

Sexual abuse of a person incapable of resistance

Whoever commits a relevant sexual act with an unconscious or incapable person, particularly vulnerable by reason of illness, physical or mental disability, taking advantage of their state of incapacity is punished with imprisonment from 4 to 12 years.

Article 180 Sexual fraud

1. Whoever, fraudulently taking advantage of an error about their personal identity, has with another person vaginal intercourse, anal intercourse or oral intercourse or a relevant sexual act is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years.
2. Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

Article 181 Sexual exhibitionism

1. Whoever publicly importunes another person with the commission of acts of a sexual nature is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.
2. The same penalty is incurred by those who, in front of another person, commit vaginal intercourse, anal intercourse or oral intercourse, against the will of that person and even if in private.
3. Attempt is punishable.
4. Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

SECTION V COMMON PROVISIONS

Article 182 Aggravation

1. The penalties provided for in section II to section IV of this chapter are aggravated by one third in their minimum and maximum limits, if:
a) The victim was less than 12 years of age at the time of commission of the facts;
b) The offender transmitted to the victim venereal disease, syphilitic disease or acquired immunodeficiency syndrome;
c) As a consequence of the facts the victim attempts or consummates suicide or death results;
d) The victim is a descendant, collateral, family member or relative by affinity up to the second degree, adoptee of the offender or person who lives with them in analogous conditions or there exists a hierarchical, economic or employment dependence.
2. In the case where several of the circumstances set out in the previous number concur, only one is relevant as

modifying the abstract framework of the legal type, with the remainder being valued in the determination of the concrete penalty.

CHAPTER V AGAINST PRIVATE LIFE

Article 183 Invasion of privacy

1. Any person who by any means, even lawful, obtains knowledge of facts relating to the intimacy of the private or sexual life of another person and, without consent, publicly divulges them without just cause, is punished with imprisonment up to 1 year or a fine.
2. Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

Article 184 Violation of secrecy

1. Whoever, without consent, reveals another's secret of which they have obtained knowledge by reason of their status, office, employment, profession or art, is punished with imprisonment up to 1 year or a fine.
2. If the secret relates to commercial, industrial, professional or artistic activity, of which the offender has obtained knowledge in the circumstances described previously, and causes harm to another person or to the State, the penalty is 2 years imprisonment or a fine.
3. Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

Article 185 Violation of domicile

1. Whoever, without consent, enters the dwelling of another person or, authorized to enter, remains there after being ordered to leave, is punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.
2. If the offender, to more easily commit the crime, takes advantage of the night, the fact that the dwelling is situated in a remote place, that there are 3 or more persons committing the act, uses a weapon, uses violence or threat of violence or

acts by means of climbing, breaking in or false key, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.
3. If there are persons inside the dwelling when the offender commits the crime, the penalty provided for in the previous number aggravated by one third in its limits is applicable.
4. Attempt is punishable.
5. Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

Article 186

Entry into other places closed to the public

1. Whoever commits the acts described in numbers 1 and 2 of the previous article in relation to any other closed or restricted place not freely accessible to the public, is punished, respectively, with the penalties referred to in those numbers reduced by half in the maximum limits.
2. Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

Artigo 187º

Violation of correspondence or telecommunications

1.	Whoever, without consent or outside procedurally admissible cases, opens a package, letter or any other writing intended for another person, or takes knowledge of its content, or prevents it from being received by its addressee, is punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.
2.	The same penalty is incurred by whoever, in the same circumstances, interferes with or takes knowledge of the content of telephone, telegraph or any other means of telecommunication communication.
3.	Whoever discloses the content of letters, packages, sealed writings, telephone calls or other communications referred to in the preceding numbers, is punished with imprisonment up to 1 year or a fine, even if he had knowledge of that content lawfully.
4.	If the acts referred to in the preceding numbers are committed by an employee of postal, telegraph, telephone or telecommunications services, the penalties are increased by one third in their limits.

5.	Criminal proceedings depend on complaint.

TÍTULO III

CRIMES AGAINST LIFE IN DEMOCRACY

CAPÍTULO I

AGAINST PUBLIC TRANQUILITY

Artigo 188º Criminal association

1.	Whoever promotes or founds a group, organization or association whose purpose or activity is directed to the commission of crimes, is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years.
2.	A criminal group, organization or association is considered to be any grouping of two or more persons who, during a period of time and acting in concert, aim to commit or incite the commission of crimes, with the intention of disturbing public order or obtaining directly or indirectly a benefit or advantage.
3.	Whoever joins, supports or participates in any of the activities of criminal groups, organizations or associations, is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 6 years.
4.	Whoever leads or directs the groups, organizations or associations referred to in the preceding numbers is punished with imprisonment from 4 to 12 years.
5.	The penalties referred to may be extraordinarily mitigated if the agent prevents or seriously endeavors to prevent the continuation of the groups, organizations or associations, or communicates their existence to the authority so that it can prevent the commission of crimes.

Artigo 189º Instigation to commit crime

1.	Whoever, publicly and by any means, incites the commission of a crime is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.

2.	Whoever, privately or publicly, praises or rewards someone who has committed a crime in such a way as, with such conduct, to incite the commission of identical crimes is punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.
3.	If in the case of the preceding numbers the crime whose commission the agent had instigated comes to be committed, the penalty of imprisonment is from 2 to 5 years, if a more serious one does not correspond to him by virtue of another legal provision.

Artigo 190º Participation in riot

1.	Whoever takes part in a public riot, during which violence against persons or property is committed collectively, is punished with imprisonment of 1 year or a fine, if no more serious penalty is due to him for participation in the crime committed.
2.	If the agent provoked or directed the riot, he is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years.
3.	If the riot is armed, the penalties referred to in the preceding numbers are doubled in their limits.

Artigo 191º

Impediment of the exercise of political rights

Whoever prevents another, through violence or threat, from exercising his political rights, is punished with imprisonment up to 1 year or a fine.

Artigo 192º Traffic in influence

1.	Whoever, by himself or through an intermediary, with his consent or ratification, solicits or accepts, for himself or for a third party, a patrimonial or non-patrimonial advantage, or its promise, to abuse his influence, real or supposed, with any public entity, is punished:
a)	With imprisonment from 2 to 6 years, if a more serious penalty is not due to him by virtue of another legal provision, if the purpose is to obtain any favorable unlawful decision;

b)	With imprisonment up to 1 year or with a fine, if a more serious penalty is not due to him by virtue of another legal provision, if the purpose is to obtain any favorable lawful decision.
2.	Whoever, by himself or through an intermediary, with his consent or ratification, gives or promises a patrimonial or non-patrimonial advantage to the persons referred to in the preceding number for the purposes provided for in paragraph a) of the preceding number is punished with imprisonment up to 4 years or with a fine.

Artigo 193º Disobedience to order of dispersal

1.	Whoever does not obey a lawful order to withdraw from a gathering or public meeting, given by a competent authority, with warning that disobedience constitutes a crime, is punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.
2.	If the disobedient person is the promoter of the meeting or gathering, he is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.

Artigo 194º

Abuse of public signals or uniform

1.	Whoever abusively uses an alarm or distress signal or call, or falsely makes it believed that assistance from others is necessary by virtue of disaster, danger or situation of collective necessity, is punished with imprisonment up to 1 year or with a fine.
2.	The same penalty is incurred by whoever unduly or abusively uses uniforms, dress, attire or insignia identifying activities, authorities or public or international institutions as a means of more easily committing any unlawful act.

Artigo 195º Usurpation of functions

1.	Whoever, without being authorized to do so, exercises functions or performs acts proper to an official, military command or public security force, arrogating to himself, expressly or tacitly, that quality, shall be punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.
2.	The same penalty is incurred by whoever exercises a profession for which the law requires a title or fulfillment of certain conditions, arrogating to himself, expressly or tacitly, to possess it or fulfill them, when, in fact, he does not possess it or does not fulfill them.
3.	The same penalty is incurred by whoever continues in the exercise of public functions, after having been officially notified of dismissal or suspension of those functions.

CAPÍTULO II

AGAINST THE SECURITY OF THE STATE

Artigo 196º Treason to the Homeland

Whoever, by means of violence, threat of violence, usurpation or abuse of functions of sovereignty, prevents or attempts to prevent the exercise of national sovereignty in the territory or in part of the territory of Timor-Leste or endangers the integrity of the national territory, as a form of submission or surrender to foreign sovereignty, is punished with imprisonment from 15 to 30 years.

Artigo 197º

Service or collaboration with enemy armed forces

1.	The Timorese citizen who collaborates with a foreign country or groups or with their representatives, or who serves under the flag of a foreign country during war or armed action against Timor-Leste, is punished with imprisonment from 12 to 25 years.
2.	Preparatory acts relating to the facts described in the preceding number are punished with imprisonment from 5 to 15 years
3.	Whoever, being Timorese or resident in the national territory, performs acts adequate to help or facilitate

any armed action or war against Timor-Leste by a foreign country or group, is punished with imprisonment from 10 to 20 years.

Artigo 198º

Sabotage against national defense

Whoever, with the intention of harming or endangering national defense, destroys, damages or renders unusable, totally or partially:
a)	Works or materials proper to or assigned to the armed forces;
b)	Routes or means of communication or transport;
c)	Any other installations related to communications or transport; or
d)	Factories or warehouses;
is punished with imprisonment from 5 to 15 years.

Artigo 199º

Campaign against peace efforts

Anyone who, being Timorese or resident in national territory, in time of preparation or war, disseminates by any means, so as to make public, rumours or statements, their own or others', which they know to be, totally or partially, false, in order to prejudice the peace efforts of Timor-Leste or to assist the foreign enemy, is punished with imprisonment from 5 to 15 years.

Artigo 200º

Violation of State secret

1. Whoever, endangering the interest of the Timorese State relating to its external security or the conduct of its foreign policy, transmits, makes accessible to an unauthorised person or makes public any fact, document, plan, object, knowledge or any other information that should, by reason of that interest, remain secret in relation to a foreign country, is punished with imprisonment from 3 to 10 years

2. Whoever collaborates with a foreign government or group with the intention of committing the acts referred to in the preceding number or recruits or assists another person charged with committing them, is punished with the penalty provided for in the preceding number.
3. If the agent who commits the acts described in the preceding numbers exercises any political, public or military function which, by its nature, should inhibit them from committing such acts more strongly than the ordinary citizen, they are punished with imprisonment from 5 to 15 years.

Artigo 201º Diplomatic infidelity

Anyone who, officially representing the Timorese State, with the intention of prejudicing national rights or interests:
a) Conducts State business with a foreign government or international organisation; or
b) Assumes commitments in the name of Timor-Leste without being duly authorised to do so;
is punished with imprisonment from 5 to 15 years.

Artigo 202º Alteration of the Rule of Law

1. Whoever, by means of violence, threat of violence or incitement to civil war, attempts to destroy, alter or subjugate the constitutionally established rule of law, is punished with imprisonment from 5 to 15 years.
2. If the act described above is committed by means of armed violence the penalty is from 5 to 20 years' imprisonment.
3. Public incitement or the distribution of weapons for the commission of the acts described above is, respectively, punished with the penalty corresponding to an attempt.

Artigo 203º

Attack against the highest representative of a sovereign body

1. Whoever attacks the life, physical integrity or freedom of the highest representative of a sovereign body or of whoever constitutionally replaces them or of whoever has been elected or appointed to the office, even before taking office, is punished with imprisonment from 8 to 20 years.
2. In case of consummation of a crime against life, physical integrity or freedom, the agent is punished with imprisonment from 12 to 25 years.
3. The penalties provided for in the preceding numbers are correspondingly applicable whenever the acts described therein are committed against a foreign person who is in the situation referred to in number 1, ambassadors and holders of leading bodies of international organisations, when they are in Timor-Leste.

Artigo 204º

Coercion against constitutional bodies

1. Whoever, by violence or threat of violence, prevents or constrains the free exercise of the functions of a sovereign body is punished with imprisonment from 3 to 10 years.
2. If the acts described in the preceding number are committed against district or local government bodies the penalty is from 2 to 6 years' imprisonment.
3. If the acts referred to described in number 1 are committed against members of those bodies the penalties referred to in numbers 1 and 2 are reduced to half their limits.

Artigo 205º

Disturbance of the functioning of a constitutional body

Anyone who, by means of tumults, disorders or shouting, unlawfully disturbs the functioning of the bodies referred to in the preceding article or the exercise of functions by any of the persons who are part of them, is punished with imprisonment up to 1 year or a fine.

Artigo 206º

Outrage of national symbols

Whoever, publicly, by words, by gestures or dissemination of writings, or by any other means of communication with the public, outrages the flag or the national anthem, the arms or emblems of Timorese sovereignty or fails to show the respect due to them, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.

CAPÍTULO III

AGAINST LIFE IN SOCIETY

SECÇÃO I

CRIMES OF COMMON DANGER

Artigo 207º Driving without a licence

Whoever uses a motorised vehicle without being qualified with the legally required licence, is punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.

Artigo 208º

Driving under the influence of alcohol or psychotropic substances

1. Whoever, at least through negligence, drives a motorised vehicle with more than 1.2mg of alcohol per litre of blood, is punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.
2. The same penalty is incurred by whoever, at least through negligence, drives a motorised vehicle, not being in a condition to do so safely, by being under the influence of a narcotic, psychotropic substance or product with an analogous disturbing effect on physical, mental or psychological aptitude.

Artigo 209º Dangerous driving

1. Whoever drives any motorised vehicle on a public road and, by not being in a condition to do so safely or by grossly violating the rules of

road traffic, creates danger to the life or physical integrity of another is punished with imprisonment from 1 to 4 years.
2. Negligence in relation to the conduct or the danger is punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.

Artigo 210º

Attack against the safety of transport

1. Whoever commits any act capable of causing the lack or diminution of safety in a means of transport and, in this way, creates a danger to the life or physical integrity of another person, is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years.
2. The provisions of number 2 of the preceding article are correspondingly applicable.

Artigo 211º Prohibited weapons

1. Anyone who, outside legal prescriptions, manufactures, imports, transports, sells or transfers to another firearms, chemical weapons, biological weapons, nuclear weapons, ammunition for them, substances for their manufacture or operation or any other type of explosive, is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 6 years.
2. If the acts described in the preceding number have as their purpose use for warlike purposes the penalty is from 2 to 8 years' imprisonment.
3. The mere possession, use or carrying of a firearm without the agent being legally authorised, is punishable with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.

Artigo 212º Drunkenness and intoxication

1. Whoever, at least through negligence, places themselves in a state of non-imputability derived from the ingestion or consumption of alcoholic drink or toxic substance and in that state commits an unlawful typical act is punished with imprisonment up to 5 years or a fine.

2. The penalty cannot exceed that provided for the unlawful typical act committed.
3. Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

Artigo 213º

Qualification for the exercise of certain activities

1. Whoever, without being legally qualified, sells, administers, prescribes or transfers in any way, habitually, to other persons, pharmaceutical products or others whose trade and prescription, administration or transfer, are reserved to health professionals or other duly licensed entities, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.
2. If as a consequence of the preceding acts danger to the life of another person results the penalty is from 1 to 4 years' imprisonment.

Artigo 214º

Produtos adulterados ou deteriorados

1.	Whoever places for sale, administers or transfers in any form to another person food products, pharmaceuticals, or others which, being deteriorated, adulterated or contaminated, are likely to endanger life is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years.
2.	If from the commission of the acts described in the preceding number results the death of the victim by reason of the consumption of such products, the penalty is from 3 to 12 years.

SECÇÃO II

CRIMES CONTRA O AMBIENTE

Artigo 215º Contra o ambiente

1.	Whoever, not observing legal or regulatory provisions protecting the environment, causes or carries out directly or indirectly emissions, discharges, radiations, extractions or excavations, ignitions, noises, vibrations, injections or deposits, in the atmosphere, in the soil,

in the subsoil or in terrestrial, maritime or underground waters, including in transboundary zones, or water abstraction that may seriously harm the balance of natural systems, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.
2.	If the agent intentionally releases, emits or introduces ionizing radiation or other substances into the air, land or maritime, continental, surface or underground waters, in a quantity that produces in one or more persons serious bodily injury requiring medical or surgical treatment or produces irreversible sequelae, he is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years and if death is caused the penalty is from 5 to 15 years of imprisonment.

Artigo 216º Agravação

1.	If the acts or activities provided for in the preceding article are committed by an industrial or commercial establishment and any of the following circumstances occurs:
a)	The industry or commercial activity operates clandestinely without the proper license or administrative authorization
b)	When express orders of the competent administrative authority for the correction or suspension of the activities referred to in the preceding article have been disobeyed
c)	When the safety rules or procedures provided for in legal or regulatory provisions have not been complied with.
d)	When, by any means, the inspection process of the competent administrative authority has been intentionally impeded or information about the environmental consequences of the industry or commercial activity has been omitted or falsified.
